```text
{
  "type": "agent_listing_delta",
  "addedTypes": [
    "claude",
    "claude-code-guide",
    "Explore",
    "general-purpose",
    "Plan",
    "statusline-setup"
  ],
  "addedLines": [
    "- claude: Catch-all for any task that doesn't fit a more specific agent. FleetView's default when no agent name is typed. (Tools: *)",
    "- claude-code-guide: Use this agent when the user asks questions (\"Can Claude...\", \"Does Claude...\", \"How do I...\") about: (1) Claude Code (the CLI tool) - features, hooks, slash commands, MCP servers, settings, IDE integrations, keyboard shortcuts; (2) Claude Agent SDK - building custom agents; (3) Claude API (formerly Anthropic API) - API usage, tool use, Anthropic SDK usage. **IMPORTANT:** Before spawning a new agent, check if there is already a running or recently completed claude-code-guide agent that you can continue via SendMessage. (Tools: Bash, Read, WebFetch, WebSearch)",
    "- Explore: Fast read-only search agent for locating code. Use it to find files by pattern (eg. \"src/components/**/*.tsx\"), grep for symbols or keywords (eg. \"API endpoints\"), or answer \"where is X defined / which files reference Y.\" Do NOT use it for code review, design-doc auditing, cross-file consistency checks, or open-ended analysis — it reads excerpts rather than whole files and will miss content past its read window. When calling, specify search breadth: \"quick\" for a single targeted lookup, \"medium\" for moderate exploration, or \"very thorough\" to search across multiple locations and naming conventions. (Tools: All tools except Agent, Artifact, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
    "- general-purpose: General-purpose agent for researching complex questions, searching for code, and executing multi-step tasks. When you are searching for a keyword or file and are not confident that you will find the right match in the first few tries use this agent to perform the search for you. (Tools: *)",
    "- Plan: Software architect agent for designing implementation plans. Use this when you need to plan the implementation strategy for a task. Returns step-by-step plans, identifies critical files, and considers architectural trade-offs. (Tools: All tools except Agent, Artifact, ExitPlanMode, Edit, Write, NotebookEdit)",
    "- statusline-setup: Use this agent to configure the user's Claude Code status line setting. (Tools: Read, Edit)"
  ],
  "removedTypes": [],
  "isInitial": true,
  "showConcurrencyNote": false
}
```

```text
  "content": "- agentic-user-journey-testing: Use when validating app, UI, or product-surface work through the running app, especially when logs/tests pass but a user job may still be blocked or unclear\n- brainstorming: Use before substantial product, UI, feature, or behavior work with ambiguity, user-journey impact, or multiple plausible approaches\n- building-react-apps: Use when building, refactoring, planning, or debugging React or Next.js apps, React components, hooks, routing, frontend state, SSR, hydration, bundles, imports, or UI settings\n- client-server-state-invariants: Use when planning, building, reviewing, or debugging React apps with server state, optimistic UI, caches, polling, SSE/WebSocket streams, background jobs, persistence, or client/server synchronization\n- designing-settings-dialogs: Use when designing, planning, building, or reviewing settings dialogs, settings dialogues, preferences panels, configuration modals, option tabs, or UI settings surfaces\n- dispatching-parallel-agents: Use when facing 2+ independent tasks that can be worked on without shared state or sequential dependencies\n- executing-plans: Use when you have a written implementation plan to execute inline with review checkpoints\n- find-skills: Helps users discover and install agent skills when they ask questions like \"how do I do X\", \"find a skill for X\", \"is there a skill that can...\", or express interest in extending capabilities. This skill should be used when the user is looking for functionality that might exist as an installable skill.\n- implementing-react-i18n: Use when adding, fixing, or reviewing React internationalization, language selectors, locale resources, translated UI copy, or bugs where changing language does not update user-facing strings\n- improve: Survey any codebase as a senior advisor and produce prioritized, self-contained implementation plans for OTHER models/agents to execute. Strictly read-only on source code — never implements, fixes, or refactors anything itself. Use when asked to audit a codebase, find improvement opportunities (bugs, security, performance, test coverage, tech debt, migrations, DX), suggest features or where to take the project next (roadmap, product direction), or generate handoff plans for another agent to implement.\n- instrumenting-control-planes: Use when building, planning, reviewing, or debugging apps with backend decisions, agent workflows, long-running jobs, SSE/WebSocket streams, recovery paths, deletes, retries, or user-visible failures\n- learning-from-bugs: Use when a meaningful bug, regression, architecture flaw, storage issue, memory problem, persistence failure, control-plane failure, or system-level failure has been diagnosed or fixed\n- optimizing-react-next-apps: Use when a React or Next.js app feels slow, cluttered, overbundled, slow to compile, slow to load, ineffective at SSR, hydration-heavy, or likely burdened by unnecessary imports or UI dependencies\n- receiving-code-review: Use when receiving code review feedback, before implementing suggestions, especially if feedback seems unclear or technically questionable - requires technical rigor and verification, not performative agreement or blind implementation\n- requesting-code-review: Use when completing tasks, implementing major features, or before merging to verify work meets requirements\n- second-opinion: Use when writing or hardening substantial specs, implementation plans, architecture plans, design plans, or unusually hard creative decisions before execution\n- subagent-driven-development\n- systematic-debugging: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes\n- test-driven-development\n- using-ultrapowers\n- verification-before-completion\n- writing-plans\n- writing-skills\n- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- verify: Verify that a code change actually does what it's supposed to by running the app and observing behavior. Use when asked to verify a PR, confirm a fix works, test a change manually, check that a feature works, or validate local changes before pushing.\n- code-review: Review the current diff for correctness bugs and reuse/simplification/efficiency cleanups at the given effort level (low/medium: fewer, high-confidence findings; high→max: broader coverage, may include uncertain findings; ultra: deep multi-agent review in the cloud). Pass --comment to post findings as inline PR comments, or --fix to apply the findings to the working tree after the review.\n- simplify: Review the changed code for reuse, simplification, efficiency, and altitude cleanups, then apply the fixes. Quality only — it does not hunt for bugs; use /code-review for that.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled cloud agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring cloud agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Reference for the Claude API / Anthropic SDK — model ids, pricing, params, streaming, tool use, MCP, agents, caching, token counting, model migration.\nTRIGGER — read BEFORE opening the target file; don't skip because it \"looks like a one-liner\" — whenever: the prompt names Claude/Anthropic in any form (Claude, Anthropic, Fable, Opus, Sonnet, Haiku, `anthropic`, `@anthropic-ai`, `claude-*`, `us.anthropic.*`, `[1m]`); the user asks about an LLM (pricing/model choice/limits/caching) — never answer from memory; OR the task is LLM-shaped with provider unstated (agent/MCP/tool-definition/multi-agent/RAG/LLM-judge/computer-use; generate/summarize/extract/classify/rewrite/converse over NL; debugging refusals/cutoffs/streaming/tool-calls/tokens).\nSKIP only when another provider is being worked on (overrides all triggers): OpenAI/GPT/Gemini/Llama/Mistral/Cohere/Ollama named in the query; OR `grep -rE 'openai|langchain_openai|google.generativeai|genai|mistralai|cohere|ollama'` over the project hits (run this grep FIRST if no provider named — don't Read the file).\n- run: Launch and drive this project's app to see a change working. Use when asked to run, start, or screenshot the app, or to confirm a change works in the real app (not just tests). First looks for a project skill that already covers launching the app; otherwise falls back to built-in patterns per project type (CLI, server, TUI, Electron, browser-driven, library).\n- init\n- review\n- security-review",
```

```text
1	/**
2	 * Shared predicates for recognizing when an Omni request already points at an
3	 * existing plan/spec (so it can skip the interactive planning phase and go
4	 * straight to supervised implementation). Imported by both the CLI arg parser
5	 * (`src/server/cli/options.ts`) and conversation creation
6	 * (`src/server/conversations/modes.ts`) so the two never drift.
7	 */
8	
9	export function looksLikePlanPath(value: string) {
10	  const trimmed = value.trim();
11	  return trimmed.includes("/") || trimmed.endsWith(".md") || trimmed.endsWith(".txt");
12	}
13	
14	/**
15	 * True when an Omni command should be treated as "a plan already exists" — i.e.
16	 * the user explicitly asked to implement, or handed over a plan/spec file path.
17	 */
18	export function omniRequestIsPlanReady(command: string) {
19	  const trimmed = command.trim();
20	  if (!trimmed) return false;
21	  if (trimmed.toLowerCase().startsWith("implement ")) return true;
22	  // A bare single-token path (no spaces) that looks like a file reference.
23	  return !/\s/.test(trimmed) && looksLikePlanPath(trimmed);
24	}
25
```

```text
1	import { createHash } from "crypto";
2	import fs from "fs";
3	import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
4	import { db } from "@/server/db";
5	import { runs } from "@/server/db/schema";
6	import { parsePlan } from "@/server/plans/parser";
7	import {
8	  assessPlanStructure,
9	  describeStructuralGaps,
10	  structureHasBlockingGaps,
11	  type PlanStructuralFacts,
12	} from "@/server/plans/readiness";
13	import {
14	  assessPlanReadinessWithLLM,
15	  type PlanReadinessVerdict,
16	} from "@/server/plans/readiness-llm";
17	import { notifyEventStreamSubscribers } from "@/server/events/live-updates";
18	
19	export type PlanReadinessAnalysisStatus =
20	  | "analyzing"
21	  | "ready"
22	  | "fallback";
23	
24	export interface PlanReadinessRecord {
25	  status: PlanReadinessAnalysisStatus;
26	  planPath: string;
27	  planHash: string;
28	  specHash: string | null;
29	  structure: PlanStructuralFacts;
30	  verdict: PlanReadinessVerdict | null;
31	  fallbackHeadline: string | null;
32	  error: string | null;
33	  generatedAt: number;
34	}
35	
36	interface VerdictMap {
37	  [planHash: string]: PlanReadinessRecord;
38	}
39	
40	const inFlight = new Map<string, Promise<PlanReadinessRecord>>();
41	
42	// Cached "analyzing" records older than this are treated as crashed and the
43	// pipeline re-issues the call.
44	const STALE_ANALYZING_MS = 60_000;
45	// Minimum interval between LLM calls per run, regardless of plan hash, to
46	// protect against runaway flushes producing many distinct hashes in a burst.
47	const RUN_RATE_LIMIT_MS = 5_000;
48	
49	const lastCallByRun = new Map<string, number>();
50	
51	function hashString(value: string) {
52	  return createHash("sha256").update(value).digest("hex");
53	}
54	
55	export function hashPlanMarkdown(markdown: string) {
56	  return hashString(markdown);
57	}
58	
59	function inflightKey(runId: string, planHash: string) {
60	  return `${runId}:${planHash}`;
61	}
62	
63	function readFileSafely(filePath: string | null | undefined): string | null {
64	  if (!filePath) return null;
65	  try {
66	    if (!fs.existsSync(filePath)) return null;
67	    return fs.readFileSync(filePath, "utf8");
68	  } catch {
69	    return null;
70	  }
71	}
72	
73	function parseVerdictMap(value: string | null | undefined): VerdictMap {
74	  if (!value?.trim()) return {};
75	  try {
76	    const parsed = JSON.parse(value);
77	    if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
78	      return parsed as VerdictMap;
79	    }
80	  } catch {
81	    // fall through to empty
82	  }
83	  return {};
84	}
85	
86	export function deriveFallbackHeadline(structure: PlanStructuralFacts, gaps: string[]): string {
87	  if (structure.itemCount === 0) {
88	    return "Plan file has no checklist items — open it, add concrete tasks, and try again.";
89	  }
90	
91	  const concerns: string[] = [];
92	  if (structure.itemsWithStubTitle.length > 0) {
93	    concerns.push(`${structure.itemsWithStubTitle.length} item(s) are stubs`);
94	  }
95	  if (structure.itemsMissingVerify.length === structure.itemCount && !structure.hasAcceptanceCriteria) {
96	    concerns.push("no Verify lines and no acceptance criteria");
97	  } else if (structure.itemsMissingVerify.length > 0 && !structure.hasAcceptanceCriteria) {
98	    concerns.push(`${structure.itemsMissingVerify.length} item(s) have no Verify line`);
99	  }
100	  if (structure.itemsWithVagueTitle.length > 0) {
101	    concerns.push(`${structure.itemsWithVagueTitle.length} item(s) have vague titles`);
102	  }
103	
104	  if (concerns.length === 0 && gaps.length === 0) {
105	    return "Plan detected — review the file below before starting implementation.";
106	  }
107	
108	  if (concerns.length === 0) {
109	    return `Plan detected — ${gaps[0]}`;
110	  }
111	
112	  return `Plan detected — ${concerns.join("; ")}. Review before implementation.`;
113	}
114	
115	export function readinessRecordIsReady(record: PlanReadinessRecord | null | undefined): boolean {
116	  if (!record) return false;
117	  if (record.status === "analyzing") return false;
118	  if (record.verdict) {
119	    return record.verdict.verdict === "ready";
120	  }
121	  return !structureHasBlockingGaps(record.structure);
122	}
123	
124	export function readinessRecordHeadline(record: PlanReadinessRecord | null | undefined): string | null {
125	  if (!record) return null;
126	  if (record.verdict) return record.verdict.headline;
127	  return record.fallbackHeadline;
128	}
129	
130	export async function loadCachedReadinessRecord(args: {
131	  runId: string;
132	  planPath: string;
133	  planHash: string;
134	}): Promise<PlanReadinessRecord | null> {
135	  const row = await db.select({ json: runs.plannerReadinessVerdictJson }).from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId)).get();
136	  const map = parseVerdictMap(row?.json ?? null);
137	  const cached = map[args.planHash];
138	  if (cached && cached.planPath === args.planPath) {
139	    return cached;
140	  }
141	  return null;
142	}
143	
144	async function persistReadinessRecord(args: { runId: string; record: PlanReadinessRecord }) {
145	  const row = await db
146	    .select({
147	      verdictJson: runs.plannerReadinessVerdictJson,
148	      artifactsJson: runs.plannerArtifactsJson,
149	    })
150	    .from(runs)
151	    .where(eq(runs.id, args.runId))
152	    .get();
153	  const map = parseVerdictMap(row?.verdictJson ?? null);
154	
155	  // Keep map bounded — only retain entries that are not the newest one for each plan path.
156	  const trimmed: VerdictMap = {};
157	  const entries = Object.entries(map);
158	  // Sort newest first so we keep recency.
159	  entries.sort((a, b) => b[1].generatedAt - a[1].generatedAt);
160	  let kept = 0;
161	  for (const [hash, record] of entries) {
162	    if (kept >= 16) break;
163	    trimmed[hash] = record;
164	    kept += 1;
165	  }
166	  trimmed[args.record.planHash] = args.record;
167	
168	  // Also patch the candidate's readinessRecord inside plannerArtifactsJson so
169	  // the artifacts panel sees the latest verdict without waiting for a fresh
170	  // refreshPlanningArtifactsForRun pass.
171	  const artifactsJson = patchArtifactsJsonWithRecord(row?.artifactsJson ?? null, args.record);
172	
173	  await db.update(runs).set({
174	    plannerReadinessVerdictJson: JSON.stringify(trimmed),
175	    ...(artifactsJson !== null ? { plannerArtifactsJson: artifactsJson } : {}),
176	    updatedAt: new Date(),
177	  }).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId));
178	}
179	
180	function patchArtifactsJsonWithRecord(
181	  artifactsJson: string | null | undefined,
182	  record: PlanReadinessRecord,
183	): string | null {
184	  if (!artifactsJson?.trim()) return null;
185	  let parsed: { candidates?: Array<{ path?: string; readinessRecord?: unknown }> };
186	  try {
187	    parsed = JSON.parse(artifactsJson);
188	  } catch {
189	    return null;
190	  }
191	  const candidates = Array.isArray(parsed.candidates) ? parsed.candidates : null;
192	  if (!candidates) return null;
193	  let mutated = false;
194	  for (const candidate of candidates) {
195	    if (candidate?.path === record.planPath) {
196	      candidate.readinessRecord = record;
197	      mutated = true;
198	    }
199	  }
200	  return mutated ? JSON.stringify(parsed) : null;
201	}
202	
203	export interface BuildReadinessArgs {
204	  runId: string;
205	  planPath: string;
206	  planMarkdown: string;
207	  specPath: string | null;
208	  specMarkdown: string | null;
209	}
210	
211	function buildBaseRecord(args: BuildReadinessArgs): Omit<PlanReadinessRecord, "status" | "verdict" | "fallbackHeadline" | "error"> & {
212	  fallbackHeadline: string;
213	} {
214	  const parsed = parsePlan(args.planMarkdown);
215	  const structure = assessPlanStructure(parsed);
216	  const gaps = describeStructuralGaps(parsed, structure);
217	  return {
218	    planPath: args.planPath,
219	    planHash: hashString(args.planMarkdown),
220	    specHash: args.specMarkdown ? hashString(args.specMarkdown) : null,
221	    structure,
222	    fallbackHeadline: deriveFallbackHeadline(structure, gaps),
223	    generatedAt: Date.now(),
224	  };
225	}
226	
227	export function buildStructuralReadinessRecord(args: BuildReadinessArgs): PlanReadinessRecord {
228	  const base = buildBaseRecord(args);
229	  return {
230	    ...base,
231	    status: "fallback",
232	    verdict: null,
233	    error: null,
234	  };
235	}
236	
237	export async function ensureReadinessVerdict(args: BuildReadinessArgs): Promise<PlanReadinessRecord> {
238	  const base = buildBaseRecord(args);
239	
240	  const cached = await loadCachedReadinessRecord({
241	    runId: args.runId,
242	    planPath: args.planPath,
243	    planHash: base.planHash,
244	  });
245	  if (cached) {
246	    // "ready" verdicts are final, "fallback" entries are returned as-is to
247	    // avoid hammering the LLM when it is unavailable, and "analyzing" usually
248	    // means another caller has a request in flight. The exception: an
249	    // "analyzing" record older than STALE_ANALYZING_MS means a previous
250	    // process exited mid-call and never finalized — re-trigger.
251	    if (cached.status !== "analyzing" || Date.now() - cached.generatedAt <= STALE_ANALYZING_MS) {
252	      return cached;
253	    }
254	  }
255	
256	  const key = inflightKey(args.runId, base.planHash);
257	  const existing = inFlight.get(key);
258	  if (existing) {
259	    return existing;
260	  }
261	
262	  const lastCall = lastCallByRun.get(args.runId) ?? 0;
263	  if (Date.now() - lastCall < RUN_RATE_LIMIT_MS) {
264	    // Too soon — return a fallback record without spending the call.
265	    return {
266	      ...base,
267	      status: "fallback",
268	      verdict: null,
269	      fallbackHeadline: base.fallbackHeadline,
270	      error: "rate_limited",
271	    };
272	  }
273	  lastCallByRun.set(args.runId, Date.now());
274	
275	  const analyzingRecord: PlanReadinessRecord = {
276	    ...base,
277	    status: "analyzing",
278	    verdict: null,
279	    fallbackHeadline: base.fallbackHeadline,
280	    error: null,
281	  };
282	
283	  await persistReadinessRecord({ runId: args.runId, record: analyzingRecord });
284	  notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
285	
286	  const promise = (async (): Promise<PlanReadinessRecord> => {
287	    const outcome = await assessPlanReadinessWithLLM({
288	      planMarkdown: args.planMarkdown,
289	      specMarkdown: args.specMarkdown,
290	      structure: base.structure,
291	    });
292	
293	    const finalized: PlanReadinessRecord = outcome.ok
294	      ? {
295	          ...base,
296	          status: "ready",
297	          verdict: outcome.verdict,
298	          fallbackHeadline: base.fallbackHeadline,
299	          error: null,
300	        }
301	      : {
302	          ...base,
303	          status: "fallback",
304	          verdict: null,
305	          fallbackHeadline: base.fallbackHeadline,
306	          error: outcome.error,
307	        };
308	
309	    await persistReadinessRecord({ runId: args.runId, record: finalized });
310	    notifyEventStreamSubscribers();
311	    return finalized;
312	  })();
313	
314	  inFlight.set(key, promise);
315	  promise.finally(() => {
316	    inFlight.delete(key);
317	  });
318	
319	  return analyzingRecord;
320	}
321	
322	export async function readinessRecordsForRun(runId: string): Promise<VerdictMap> {
323	  const row = await db.select({ json: runs.plannerReadinessVerdictJson }).from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
324	  return parseVerdictMap(row?.json ?? null);
325	}
326	
327	export async function readinessRecordForPlanFile(args: {
328	  runId: string;
329	  planPath: string;
330	}): Promise<PlanReadinessRecord | null> {
331	  const markdown = readFileSafely(args.planPath);
332	  if (!markdown) return null;
333	  const planHash = hashString(markdown);
334	  return loadCachedReadinessRecord({
335	    runId: args.runId,
336	    planPath: args.planPath,
337	    planHash,
338	  });
```

```text
1	import { getAppDataPath } from "@/server/app-root";
2	import { homedir } from "os";
3	import { join } from "path";
4	import { readdir, open } from "fs/promises";
5	
6	export function isFullAccessAgentMode(mode: string | null | undefined) {
7	  return mode === "full-access" || mode === "danger-full-access";
8	}
9	
10	export function buildGeminiArgs(input: {
11	  model?: string | null;
12	  mode?: string | null;
13	}) {
14	  const args = ["--experimental-acp"];
15	  if (isFullAccessAgentMode(input.mode)) {
16	    args.push("--approval-mode", "yolo");
17	  }
18	  const model = input.model?.trim();
19	  if (model) {
20	    args.push("--model", model);
21	  }
22	  args.push("--include-directories", getAppDataPath("attachments"));
23	  return args;
24	}
25	
26	export async function resolveFullGeminiUuid(resumeSessionId: string, customCwd?: string): Promise<string> {
27	  if (typeof resumeSessionId !== "string" || resumeSessionId.length === 36) {
28	    return resumeSessionId;
29	  }
30	
31	  // Extract the 8-character short ID from the end of the string if possible
32	  // (handles "1aba5d6a", "omniharness-1aba5d6a", etc.)
33	  let shortId = resumeSessionId;
34	  const hex8Match = resumeSessionId.match(/[-_]?([0-9a-f]{8})$/i);
35	  if (hex8Match) {
36	    shortId = hex8Match[1];
37	  }
38	
39	  if (shortId.length !== 8) {
40	    return resumeSessionId;
41	  }
42	
43	  const configRoots = [
44	    join(homedir(), ".gemini"),
45	    join(customCwd || process.cwd(), ".omniharness/cli-home/gemini/.gemini"),
46	  ];
47	
48	  for (const configRoot of configRoots) {
49	    const tmpDir = join(configRoot, "tmp");
50	    let projectDirs: string[];
51	    try {
52	      projectDirs = await readdir(tmpDir);
53	    } catch {
54	      continue;
55	    }
56	
57	    for (const dirName of projectDirs) {
58	      const chatsDir = join(tmpDir, dirName, "chats");
59	      let entries: string[];
60	      try {
61	        entries = await readdir(chatsDir);
62	      } catch {
63	        continue;
64	      }
65	
66	      for (const entryName of entries) {
67	        if (entryName.endsWith(`-${shortId}.jsonl`)) {
68	          const filePath = join(chatsDir, entryName);
69	          let fileHandle;
70	          try {
71	            fileHandle = await open(filePath, "r");
72	            const buffer = Buffer.alloc(4096);
73	            const { bytesRead } = await fileHandle.read(buffer, 0, 4096, 0);
74	            const chunk = buffer.toString("utf8", 0, bytesRead);
75	            const firstLine = chunk.split("\n")[0];
76	            if (firstLine) {
77	              const parsed = JSON.parse(firstLine);
78	              if (typeof parsed.sessionId === "string" && parsed.sessionId.length === 36) {
79	                return parsed.sessionId;
80	              }
81	            }
82	          } catch {
83	            // Ignore parse or read errors and continue
84	          } finally {
85	            await fileHandle?.close();
86	          }
87	        }
88	      }
89	    }
90	  }
91	
92	  // Fallback: Pad the short ID to a synthetic 36-character UUID
93	  // to satisfy the Gemini CLI's argument validator regex, which requires
94	  // a UUID or index. Since the CLI matches files by the first 8 characters
95	  // of the UUID, this synthetic UUID will correctly match session-*-<shortId>.jsonl.
96	  return `${shortId}-0000-0000-0000-000000000000`;
97	}
98
```

```text
1	/**
2	 * Defensive wrapper around an ACP stream that normalizes incoming
3	 * `session/update` notifications before they reach the SDK's zod
4	 * validator.
5	 *
6	 * Background: `acp.ClientSideConnection` validates every `session/update`
7	 * via `zSessionNotification.parse(params)`. When validation fails it
8	 * logs `Error handling notification` to stderr and **silently drops the
9	 * entire notification** — the agent runtime never records the
10	 * tool_call / message_chunk / thought, the worker stream stops
11	 * advancing, and the user sees a hung session even though the SDK is
12	 * still streaming. Concrete failures we've seen so far:
13	 *
14	 *   - `claude-agent-acp` emits ToolCallLocation `line` as a
15	 *     `[startLine, endLine]` tuple for range reads; the schema requires
16	 *     `uint32 | null`.
17	 *
18	 * Rather than chasing one field at a time, this sanitizer pre-validates
19	 * each notification against the SDK's own zod schema and, for any
20	 * issue, walks the path and replaces the offending value with the
21	 * closest schema-conforming approximation (first uint32 in an array
22	 * for number-or-null fields; `null` for everything else). Failures are
23	 * logged to `.omniharness/bridge-validation-failures.log` so we can
24	 * widen the salvage rules when a new field shape shows up.
25	 */
26	import { promises as fs } from "fs";
27	import path from "path";
28	import type * as acp from "@agentclientprotocol/sdk";
29	import { zSessionNotification } from "@agentclientprotocol/sdk/dist/schema/zod.gen.js";
30	import { getAppDataPath } from "@/server/app-root";
31	
32	// The SDK ships generated schemas built against zod 4's issue shape
33	// (nested `errors` arrays, etc), but our installed zod is the 3.x line
34	// where `z.ZodIssue` is the v3 type. Rather than fight the typings,
35	// treat the issue payload as opaque and pull out the two fields we
36	// rely on (`code`, `path`) defensively at runtime.
37	interface ZodIssueLike {
38	  readonly code: string;
39	  readonly path: ReadonlyArray<PropertyKey>;
40	  readonly [extra: string]: unknown;
41	}
42	
43	type AcpStream = ConstructorParameters<typeof acp.ClientSideConnection>[1];
44	
45	const FAILURE_LOG_PATH = getAppDataPath(".omniharness/bridge-validation-failures.log");
46	const MAX_UINT32 = 4_294_967_295;
47	const MAX_SALVAGE_PASSES = 8;
48	
49	export function sanitizeAcpStream(stream: AcpStream): AcpStream {
50	  const readable = new ReadableStream({
51	    async start(controller) {
52	      const reader = stream.readable.getReader();
53	      try {
54	        while (true) {
55	          const { value, done } = await reader.read();
56	          if (done) {
57	            break;
58	          }
59	          controller.enqueue(sanitizeIncomingMessage(value));
60	        }
61	      } finally {
62	        reader.releaseLock();
63	        controller.close();
64	      }
65	    },
66	  });
67	  return { readable, writable: stream.writable };
68	}
69	
70	function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
71	  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
72	}
73	
74	/**
75	 * Top-level sanitizer. For `session/update` notifications, pre-validate
76	 * `params` against the SDK schema and salvage any zod issues. For all
77	 * other messages, pass through unchanged.
78	 */
79	export function sanitizeIncomingMessage(message: unknown): unknown {
80	  if (!isRecord(message) || message.method !== "session/update") {
81	    return message;
82	  }
83	  const params = message.params;
84	  if (!isRecord(params)) {
85	    return message;
86	  }
87	
88	  // Cheap targeted pass first — fixes the known ToolCallLocation.line
89	  // tuple shape without needing to walk the zod tree.
90	  fastPathRepairs(params);
91	
92	  const initial = zSessionNotification.safeParse(params);
93	  if (initial.success) {
94	    return message;
95	  }
96	
97	  // Mutate-in-place repair loop. Each pass applies all current issues,
98	  // then re-validates. Bounded to avoid an infinite loop on
99	  // pathological inputs.
100	  const initialIssues = (initial.error.issues as unknown) as ReadonlyArray<ZodIssueLike>;
101	  let lastIssues: ReadonlyArray<ZodIssueLike> = initialIssues;
102	  for (let pass = 0; pass < MAX_SALVAGE_PASSES; pass += 1) {
103	    let changed = false;
104	    for (const issue of lastIssues) {
105	      if (applyIssueRepair(params, issue)) {
106	        changed = true;
107	      }
108	    }
109	    if (!changed) {
110	      break;
111	    }
112	    const reparsed = zSessionNotification.safeParse(params);
113	    if (reparsed.success) {
114	      void logFailure({
115	        kind: "salvaged",
116	        message,
117	        initialIssues,
118	        passes: pass + 1,
119	      });
120	      return message;
121	    }
122	    lastIssues = (reparsed.error.issues as unknown) as ReadonlyArray<ZodIssueLike>;
123	  }
124	
125	  // Couldn't fully salvage — log and pass through. acp.js will then
126	  // reject it; at least we have the full record on disk to debug.
127	  void logFailure({
128	    kind: "unsalvaged",
129	    message,
130	    initialIssues,
131	    finalIssues: lastIssues,
132	  });
133	  return message;
134	}
135	
136	/** Known patterns where a targeted rewrite is cheaper than a zod walk. */
137	function fastPathRepairs(params: Record<string, unknown>) {
138	  const update = isRecord(params.update) ? params.update : null;
139	  if (!update) return;
140	  const locations = update.locations;
141	  if (Array.isArray(locations)) {
142	    for (const loc of locations) {
143	      if (isRecord(loc) && "line" in loc) {
144	        loc.line = coerceLineField(loc.line);
145	      }
146	    }
147	  }
148	}
149	
150	function coerceLineField(value: unknown): number | null {
151	  if (value === null || value === undefined) {
152	    return null;
153	  }
154	  if (isUint32(value)) {
155	    return value;
156	  }
157	  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
158	    for (const candidate of value) {
159	      if (isUint32(candidate)) {
160	        return candidate;
161	      }
162	    }
163	  }
164	  return null;
165	}
166	
167	function isUint32(value: unknown): value is number {
168	  return typeof value === "number" && Number.isInteger(value) && value >= 0 && value <= MAX_UINT32;
169	}
170	
171	/**
172	 * Generic repair: given a zod issue, mutate the value at issue.path so
173	 * the next parse pass succeeds. Returns true if anything changed.
174	 */
175	function applyIssueRepair(root: Record<string, unknown>, issue: ZodIssueLike): boolean {
176	  const path = issue.path;
177	  if (path.length === 0) {
178	    return false;
179	  }
180	  const parent = navigateToParent(root, path);
181	  if (parent === null) {
182	    return false;
183	  }
184	  const key = path[path.length - 1] as string | number;
185	  const current = (parent as Record<string | number, unknown>)[key];
186	
187	  const replacement = chooseReplacement(issue, current);
188	  if (replacement === KEEP) {
189	    return false;
190	  }
191	  (parent as Record<string | number, unknown>)[key] = replacement;
192	  return true;
193	}
194	
195	const KEEP = Symbol("keep");
196	type Repair = unknown | typeof KEEP;
197	
198	/**
199	 * Pick a schema-conforming stand-in for a value the validator rejected.
200	 *
201	 * Heuristics in priority order:
202	 *   - Array of numbers being asked for a single number → first valid
203	 *     element (preserves a navigable location for range reads).
204	 *   - "Expected number / received string": parse as integer, else null.
205	 *   - Any other invalid_type / invalid_union: null.
206	 *   - Unrecognized keys: leave the value (parent's strict mode will
207	 *     pass after we null the field; do not delete to avoid TS issues).
208	 *
209	 * Returns KEEP when no safe replacement is available — caller leaves
210	 * it as-is and we'll log it as unsalvaged.
211	 */
212	function chooseReplacement(_issue: ZodIssueLike, current: unknown): Repair {
213	  // Refuse to null an object or array — that would strip out whole
214	  // session_update payloads (content blocks, plan entries, etc) just
215	  // because a nested leaf failed. The salvage loop pre-walks into
216	  // nested issues, so leaves get repaired individually; non-leaf
217	  // failures fall through to the unsalvaged log so we can extend the
218	  // rules deliberately rather than corrupting data.
219	  if (isRecord(current) || Array.isArray(current)) {
220	    if (Array.isArray(current)) {
221	      for (const candidate of current) {
222	        if (isUint32(candidate)) {
223	          return candidate;
224	        }
225	      }
226	    }
227	    return KEEP;
228	  }
229	  if (typeof current === "string") {
230	    const parsed = Number.parseInt(current, 10);
231	    if (Number.isFinite(parsed) && parsed >= 0 && parsed <= MAX_UINT32) {
232	      return parsed;
233	    }
234	  }
235	  // Leaf scalar that doesn't fit the schema — nulling is the safest
236	  // approximation (and matches the `uint32 | null` pattern we keep
237	  // hitting in ToolCallLocation.line and similar fields).
238	  return null;
239	}
240	
241	function navigateToParent(root: Record<string, unknown>, path: ReadonlyArray<PropertyKey>): unknown {
242	  let cursor: unknown = root;
243	  for (let i = 0; i < path.length - 1; i += 1) {
244	    if (cursor === null || cursor === undefined) {
245	      return null;
246	    }
247	    const segment = path[i];
248	    if (Array.isArray(cursor)) {
249	      if (typeof segment !== "number") return null;
250	      cursor = cursor[segment];
251	    } else if (isRecord(cursor)) {
252	      cursor = cursor[segment as string];
253	    } else {
254	      return null;
255	    }
256	  }
257	  return cursor ?? null;
258	}
259	
260	let failureLogInitPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
261	async function logFailure(payload: {
262	  kind: "salvaged" | "unsalvaged";
263	  message: unknown;
264	  initialIssues: ReadonlyArray<ZodIssueLike>;
265	  finalIssues?: ReadonlyArray<ZodIssueLike>;
266	  passes?: number;
267	}) {
268	  try {
269	    if (!failureLogInitPromise) {
270	      failureLogInitPromise = fs.mkdir(path.dirname(FAILURE_LOG_PATH), { recursive: true }).then(() => undefined);
271	    }
272	    await failureLogInitPromise;
273	    const line = JSON.stringify({
274	      ts: new Date().toISOString(),
275	      ...payload,
276	    }) + "\n";
277	    await fs.appendFile(FAILURE_LOG_PATH, line, "utf8");
278	  } catch {
279	    // Logging failures should never crash the pipeline.
280	  }
281	}
```

```text
1	import fs from "fs";
2	import path from "path";
3	import { randomUUID } from "crypto";
4	import { getAppDataPath } from "@/server/app-root";
5	
6	interface AttachmentInput {
7	  kind?: string;
8	  name?: string;
9	  path?: string;
10	  storagePath?: string;
11	  mimeType?: string;
12	  size?: number;
13	}
14	
15	function quoteBlock(text: string) {
16	  return text
17	    .split(/\r?\n/)
18	    .map((line) => `> ${line}`)
19	    .join("\n");
20	}
21	
22	function formatAttachments(attachments: AttachmentInput[]) {
23	  if (attachments.length === 0) return "";
24	
25	  const lines = attachments.map((attachment) => {
26	    const parts = [
27	      attachment.kind?.trim(),
28	      attachment.name?.trim(),
29	      attachment.mimeType?.trim(),
30	      typeof attachment.size === "number" ? `${attachment.size} bytes` : undefined,
31	      attachment.storagePath?.trim() || attachment.path?.trim(),
32	    ].filter(Boolean);
33	
34	    return parts.length > 0 ? `- ${parts.join(" | ")}` : "- attachment";
35	  });
36	
37	  return `\nAttachments:\n\n${lines.join("\n")}\n`;
38	}
39	
40	export function buildAdHocPlanMarkdown(command: string, attachments: AttachmentInput[] = []) {
41	  return `# Ad Hoc Request
42	
43	Original command:
44	
45	${quoteBlock(command)}
46	${formatAttachments(attachments)}
47	
48	## Supervisor Notes
49	
50	This file stores the original request and any attached context for the supervisor.
51	`;
52	}
53	
54	export function createAdHocPlan(command: string, attachments: AttachmentInput[] = []) {
55	  const adHocDir = getAppDataPath("vibes", "ad-hoc");
56	  fs.mkdirSync(adHocDir, { recursive: true });
57	
58	  const filename = `${new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-")}-${randomUUID()}.md`;
59	  const relativePath = path.join("vibes", "ad-hoc", filename);
60	  fs.writeFileSync(getAppDataPath(relativePath), buildAdHocPlanMarkdown(command, attachments), "utf-8");
61	  return relativePath;
62	}
63	
64	export function rewriteAdHocPlan(relativePath: string, command: string, attachments: AttachmentInput[] = []) {
65	  fs.writeFileSync(getAppDataPath(relativePath), buildAdHocPlanMarkdown(command, attachments), "utf-8");
66	}
67
```

```text
10	function getAgentScratchRoots(): string[] {
11	  const override = process.env.OMNIHARNESS_AGENT_SCRATCH_ROOTS?.trim();
12	  if (override) {
13	    return override.split(path.delimiter).map((root) => path.resolve(root)).filter(Boolean);
14	  }

17	
18	function isInsideScratchRoot(candidate: string): boolean {
19	  const resolved = path.resolve(candidate);
20	  return getAgentScratchRoots().some((root) => {
21	    const rel = path.relative(root, resolved);
22	    return rel === "" || (!rel.startsWith("..") && !path.isAbsolute(rel));
23	  });
24	}
25	
26	function extractScratchAbsolutePaths(outputText: string): string[] {
27	  const roots = getAgentScratchRoots();
28	  if (roots.length === 0) return [];
29	  const absMarkdownPattern = /\/[\w./-]+\.md\b/g;
30	  const seen = new Set<string>();
31	  for (const match of outputText.matchAll(absMarkdownPattern)) {
32	    const candidate = match[0];
33	    if (roots.some((root) => candidate === root || candidate.startsWith(root + path.sep))) {
34	      seen.add(candidate);
35	    }
36	  }
37	  return [...seen];
38	}
39	
40	function copyIntoProjectStandardDir(args: {
41	  cwd: string;
42	  kind: "spec" | "plan" | "unknown";
43	  sourcePath: string;
44	}): string | null {
45	  const subdir = args.kind === "spec" ? "specs" : "plans";
46	  const targetDir = path.join(args.cwd, "docs", "superpowers", subdir);
47	  try {
48	    fs.mkdirSync(targetDir, { recursive: true });
49	  } catch {
50	    return null;
51	  }
52	  const target = path.join(targetDir, path.basename(args.sourcePath));
53	  try {
54	    if (!fs.existsSync(target)) {
55	      fs.copyFileSync(args.sourcePath, target);
56	    }
57	  } catch {
58	    return null;
59	  }
60	  return target;

62	
63	function searchScratchByBasename(dir: string, basename: string, depth: number): string | null {
64	  if (depth < 0) return null;
65	  let entries: fs.Dirent[];
66	  try {
67	    entries = fs.readdirSync(dir, { withFileTypes: true });
68	  } catch {
69	    return null;
70	  }
71	  for (const entry of entries) {
72	    const full = path.join(dir, entry.name);
73	    if (entry.isFile() && entry.name === basename) {
74	      return full;
75	    }
76	    if (entry.isDirectory()) {
77	      const found = searchScratchByBasename(full, basename, depth - 1);
78	      if (found) return found;
79	    }
80	  }
81	  return null;
82	}
83	
84	function findScratchArtifact(basename: string): string | null {
85	  for (const root of getAgentScratchRoots()) {
86	    if (!fs.existsSync(root)) continue;
87	    const found = searchScratchByBasename(root, basename, SCRATCH_SEARCH_MAX_DEPTH);
88	    if (found) return found;
89	  }
90	  return null;
91	}

93	function relocateByBasenameFromScratch(args: {

141	function inferKind(candidatePath: string): PlannerArtifactCandidate["kind"] {

171	function extractMarkdownPaths(outputText: string) {
172	  const artifactReferencePattern = /\b(?:created|drafted|finalized|generated|handoff|prepared|saved|updated|wrote|spec_path|plan_path)\b/i;
173	  const markdownPathPattern = /(?:\/[\w./-]+|(?:docs|vibes)[\w./-]*|\.?\.?\/[\w./-]+)\.md\b/g;
174	  const matches: string[] = [];
175	  let artifactReferenceContextLines = 0;
176	
177	  for (const line of outputText.split(/\r?\n/)) {
178	    const hasArtifactReference = artifactReferencePattern.test(line);
179	    if (hasArtifactReference) {
180	      artifactReferenceContextLines = 3;
181	    }
182	
183	    if (!hasArtifactReference && artifactReferenceContextLines === 0) {
184	      continue;
185	    }
186	
187	    matches.push(...line.matchAll(markdownPathPattern).map((match) => match[0]));
188	
189	    if (!hasArtifactReference && artifactReferenceContextLines > 0) {
190	      artifactReferenceContextLines -= 1;
191	    }
192	  }
193	
194	  return Array.from(new Set(matches));
195	}
196	
197	export function extractPlannerHandoffBlock(outputText: string): PlannerHandoff | null {
198	  const matches = Array.from(outputText.matchAll(/<omniharness-plan-handoff>([\s\S]*?)<\/omniharness-plan-handoff>/gi));
199	  const match = matches.at(-1);
200	  if (!match) {
201	    return null;
202	  }
203	
204	  const lines = match[1]
205	    .split(/\r?\n/)
206	    .map((line) => line.trim())
207	    .filter(Boolean);
208	
209	  const byKey = new Map<string, string>();
210	  for (const line of lines) {
211	    const separatorIndex = line.indexOf(":");
212	    if (separatorIndex < 0) continue;
213	    const key = line.slice(0, separatorIndex).trim().toLowerCase();
214	    const value = line.slice(separatorIndex + 1).trim();
215	    byKey.set(key, value);
216	  }
217	
218	  return {
219	    specPath: byKey.get("spec_path") || null,
220	    planPath: byKey.get("plan_path") || null,
221	    ready: /^(yes|true)$/i.test(byKey.get("ready") || ""),
222	    summary: byKey.get("summary") || null,
223	  };
224	}
225	
226	async function buildCandidate(args: {
227	  cwd: string;
228	  rawPath: string;
229	  source: PlannerArtifactCandidate["source"];
230	  evidence: string;
231	  confidence: number;
232	}) {
233	  const resolvedPath = normalizeCandidatePath(args.cwd, args.rawPath);
234	  if (!resolvedPath) {
235	    return null;
236	  }
237	
238	  let finalPath = resolvedPath;
239	  let kind = inferKind(resolvedPath);
240	  let evidence = args.evidence;
241	  let exists = fs.existsSync(finalPath);
242	
243	  if (exists && isInsideScratchRoot(finalPath)) {
244	    const target = copyIntoProjectStandardDir({ cwd: args.cwd, kind, sourcePath: finalPath });
245	    if (target) {
246	      evidence = `${evidence}; relocated from agent scratch dir ${finalPath}`;
247	      finalPath = target;
248	      kind = inferKind(finalPath);
249	      exists = fs.existsSync(finalPath);
250	    }
251	  } else if (!exists && args.source === "handoff") {
252	    const relocated = relocateByBasenameFromScratch({
253	      cwd: args.cwd,
254	      kind,
255	      rawPath: args.rawPath,
256	    });
257	    if (relocated) {
258	      finalPath = relocated.path;
259	      kind = inferKind(finalPath);
260	      exists = fs.existsSync(finalPath);
261	      evidence = `${evidence}; relocated from agent scratch dir ${relocated.relocatedFrom}`;
262	    }
263	  }
264	
265	  let readiness: PlanReadinessAssessment | null = null;
266	  if (exists && kind === "plan") {
267	    const markdown = fs.readFileSync(finalPath, "utf8");
268	    readiness = await assessPlanReadiness(parsePlan(markdown));
269	  }
270	
271	  return {
272	    path: finalPath,
273	    kind,
274	    source: args.source,
275	    confidence: args.confidence,
276	    evidence,
277	    exists,
278	    readiness,
279	  } satisfies PlannerArtifactCandidate;
280	}
281	
282	export async function collectPlannerArtifacts(args: {
283	  cwd: string;
284	  outputText: string;
285	}): Promise<PlannerArtifacts> {
286	  const handoff = extractPlannerHandoffBlock(args.outputText);
287	  const candidates: PlannerArtifactCandidate[] = [];
288	  const seenPaths = new Set<string>();
289	
290	  const handoffInputs = [
291	    ...(handoff?.specPath ? [{ rawPath: handoff.specPath, source: "handoff" as const, evidence: "spec_path in handoff block", confidence: 1 }] : []),
292	    ...(handoff?.planPath ? [{ rawPath: handoff.planPath, source: "handoff" as const, evidence: "plan_path in handoff block", confidence: 1 }] : []),
293	  ];
294	  const scratchFallbackInputs = extractScratchAbsolutePaths(args.outputText).map((rawPath) => ({
295	    rawPath,
296	    source: "output_text" as const,
297	    evidence: `agent scratch path mentioned in output: ${rawPath}`,
298	    confidence: 0.8,
299	  }));
300	  const candidateInputs = [
301	    ...handoffInputs,
302	    ...(handoffInputs.length > 0
303	      ? scratchFallbackInputs
304	      : [
305	        ...extractMarkdownPaths(args.outputText).map((rawPath) => ({
306	          rawPath,
307	          source: "output_text" as const,
308	          evidence: `mentioned in output: ${rawPath}`,
309	          confidence: 0.65,
310	        })),
311	        ...scratchFallbackInputs,
312	      ]),
313	  ];
314	
315	  for (const input of candidateInputs) {
316	    const candidate = await buildCandidate({
317	      cwd: args.cwd,
318	      rawPath: input.rawPath,
319	      source: input.source,
320	      evidence: input.evidence,
321	      confidence: input.confidence,
322	    });
323	    if (!candidate || seenPaths.has(candidate.path)) {
324	      continue;
325	    }
326	    if (handoff?.ready && input.source === "handoff" && candidate.kind === "plan") {
327	      candidate.readiness = {
328	        ready: true,
329	        questions: [],
330	        gaps: [],
331	        structure: candidate.readiness?.structure ?? {
332	          itemCount: 0,
333	          hasAcceptanceCriteria: false,
334	          hasGoalSection: false,
335	          itemsMissingDetails: [],
336	          itemsMissingVerify: [],
337	          itemsWithEmptyVerify: [],
338	          itemsWithVagueTitle: [],
339	          itemsWithStubTitle: [],
340	        },
341	      };
342	    }
343	
344	    seenPaths.add(candidate.path);
345	    candidates.push(candidate);
346	  }
347	
348	  const explicitSpecCandidates = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === "spec" && candidate.exists);
349	  if (explicitSpecCandidates.length === 0) {
350	    const detectedPlanCandidates = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === "plan" && candidate.exists);
351	    for (const planCandidate of detectedPlanCandidates) {
352	      for (const inferredSpecPath of inferSiblingSpecPaths(planCandidate.path)) {
353	        if (seenPaths.has(inferredSpecPath) || !fs.existsSync(inferredSpecPath)) {
354	          continue;
355	        }
356	
357	        const candidate = await buildCandidate({
358	          cwd: args.cwd,
359	          rawPath: inferredSpecPath,
360	          source: "inferred",
361	          evidence: `inferred from plan artifact: ${planCandidate.path}`,
362	          confidence: 0.75,
363	        });
364	        if (!candidate) {
365	          continue;
366	        }
367	
368	        seenPaths.add(candidate.path);
369	        candidates.push(candidate);
370	      }
371	    }
372	  }
373	
374	  const specCandidates = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === "spec" && candidate.exists);
375	  const planCandidates = candidates.filter((candidate) => candidate.kind === "plan" && candidate.exists);
376	
377	  const specPath = handoff?.specPath && specCandidates.length > 0
378	    ? specCandidates[0]?.path ?? null
379	    : specCandidates.length === 1
380	      ? specCandidates[0]?.path ?? null
381	      : null;
382	
383	  const handoffPlanPath = handoff?.planPath
384	    ? normalizeCandidatePath(args.cwd, handoff.planPath)
385	    : null;
386	  const handoffPlanCandidate = planCandidates.find((candidate) =>
387	    candidate.source === "handoff" && (handoffPlanPath ? candidate.path === handoffPlanPath : true),
388	  ) ?? planCandidates.find((candidate) => candidate.source === "handoff");
389	  const scratchRelocatedPlanCandidate = planCandidates.find((candidate) =>
390	    candidate.evidence.includes("relocated from agent scratch dir"),
391	  );
392	  const planPath = handoffPlanCandidate
393	    ? handoffPlanCandidate.path
394	    : scratchRelocatedPlanCandidate
395	      ? scratchRelocatedPlanCandidate.path
396	      : planCandidates.length === 1
397	        ? planCandidates[0]?.path ?? null
398	        : null;
399	
400	  return { specPath, planPath, candidates };
401	}
402
```

## Claude tool result: toolu_01XkPWiYco1nerUZKZv1HSXt · 2026-06-30T22:49:55.313Z

```text
CRITICAL!!: 
- NEVER EVER EVER create a branch!!! branches are FORBIDDEN!!!
- NEVER EVER EVER delete the `bugdrop-screenshots` branch, locally or remotely. It is protected project history.
- NEVER create a worktree, UNLESS the user has specifically asked for it!
- NEVER delete files unless the user has specifically asked for it. If you think a file is misplaced, unrelated, or stale, that is an entirely separate request — ask first. "Looks unrelated to this task" is not authorization to delete.

- When the user gives a UUID and asks what is going on with it, treat it as an OmniHarness conversation/session lookup: check `sqlite.db`, starting with the `runs` row for that UUID, then inspect related `workers`, `messages`, `execution_events`, queued messages, and validation/plan records as needed.
- To delete all conversations and associated persisted artifacts, use `scripts/delete-conversations.sh`

Testing:
- When testing the app, use the already-running process if one exists instead of starting another server.
- The normal local app URL is `http://localhost:3035`; the compressed Next dev server is usually at `http://localhost:3050`.
- Clean up any test sessions/conversations and their associated persisted artifacts before finishing.
- For lifecycle / chaos-style regressions (reconnect, restart, FK-on-delete, plan-review leftover state), use `pnpm test:lifecycle`. Scenarios live under `tests/lifecycle/scenarios/` and drive the control plane via HTTP/SSE — no Chromium. To debug a specific reported bug, mirror it as a new scenario file.
- Read `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md` before adding new server-side state transitions. It is the spec all new code is held to.
- Worker conversation content (direct-control and supervisor-driven worker turns) lives in the unified worker stream — one append-only JSONL per worker at `app-data/run-data/<runId>/<workerId>.jsonl`. Persist new worker content through `appendWorkerEntry` in `src/server/workers/output-store.ts`; render it through `WorkerEntriesManager` and the `entries` prop on `Terminal`. Do NOT add a parallel persistence layer (a sibling JSONL, a new `messages.kind`, an in-memory cache that the frontend reconciles against the stream). See `docs/architecture/worker-conversation-stream.md`.

Lifecycle observability rules (full doc: `docs/architecture/lifecycle-observability-and-testing.md`):
- Every server-side decision (spawn, reattach, recreate, give up, refuse, delete, fail) emits a typed named event via `emitNamedEvent` from `@/server/events/named-events`. Silent early returns and bare `catch {}` are bugs.
- User-relevant failures additionally emit `error.surfaced` with a stable `code` (typed union in `named-events.ts`), `surface`, and at least one of `runId`/`workerId`/`conversationId`. Never funnel through a blanket wrapper in `api-errors.ts`.
- All SSE frames carry an `id:`. Clients reconnect with `Last-Event-ID`; the server replays from the ring buffer or emits `stream.resync_required`. Snapshot bootstrap is `GET /api/events?snapshot=1` (anchor id in the `x-omni-last-event-id` response header).
- Dev-only event log: `GET /api/events/log?since=<id>&runId=<id>` returns the ring buffer as JSON. Use this when triaging "X didn't happen" bug reports — if the event isn't there, the server didn't do the thing, and the next step is finding the silent branch.
- Chaos is a client-side concern. Never add fault-injection code paths to server code.

Frontend i18n:
- EVERY user-facing frontend string MUST live in `shared/locales/*.json` and be rendered with the `t()` function from `@/lib/i18n`.
- NEVER hardcode user-facing JSX text, button labels, dialog titles, aria-labels, titles, placeholders, empty states, status labels, help text, error fallback text, or visible option labels directly in components.
- For every new user-facing string, add the key to `shared/locales/en.json` and every other locale file in `shared/locales/` in the same change. Use stable dotted keys like `settings.runtime.recovery` or `conversation.sidebar.search`.
- Components that render translated strings and need to update when the language changes MUST subscribe to the i18n manager by calling `useI18nSnapshot()` from `@/lib/i18n`; then render text with the existing `t("key", params?)` function. The hook is only for re-rendering; `t()` is the translation API.
- Non-React modules that produce user-visible text must still use `t("key", params?)` at the boundary where text is produced. Protocol values, storage keys, API paths, CSS classes, model ids, and database values are not translation keys unless they are displayed as copy.
- Do not pass translated strings through transactions or persist translated UI copy. Store stable ids/values and translate at render time.

React Best Practices:
- MutableRefObject is deprecated in React; use RefObject instead.
- NEVER use require() to import modules. Use import instead.
- ALWAYS centralize all state in global single source of truth Manager classes. Components/listeners subscribe to updates from Managers and use Manager methods to update data.
- Always implement a custom data manager for any data structure. NEVER use separate state variables and arrays as the source of truth.
- NEVER send data around in transactions.
- Avoid using useEffect() to update state. Prefer useCallback() or Manager methods for explicit state transitions.
- Avoid callback hell, race conditions, and infinite loops.
- NEVER store string literals or UI settings for the frontend in the .env file. Use literals in files, inline arrays, or .json files instead.
- When debugging React: if a fix did not work, think hard about whether this is a race condition. Race conditions are common in React.
```

```text
1	import { asc, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
2	import * as acp from "@agentclientprotocol/sdk";
3	import { db } from "@/server/db";
4	import { messages, runs, workers } from "@/server/db/schema";
5	import { listExecutionEventsForRun } from "@/server/events/execution-event-store";
6	import { createConversation } from "@/server/conversations/create";
7	import { sendConversationMessage } from "@/server/conversations/send-message";
8	import { waitForEventStreamNotification } from "@/server/events/live-updates";
9	import { CONVERSATION_MODES, type ConversationMode } from "@/server/conversations/modes";
10	import { createShortUuid } from "@/server/runs/ids";
11	
12	const OMNI_ACP_MODES: acp.SessionMode[] = [
13	  {
14	    id: "implementation",
15	    name: "Implement plan",
16	    description: "Start a supervisor-managed OmniHarness implementation run.",
17	  },
18	  {
19	    id: "planning",
20	    name: "Create plan",
21	    description: "Use a single ACP-backed worker to create or refine a plan.",
22	  },
23	  {
24	    id: "direct",
25	    name: "Direct control",
26	    description: "Use a single ACP-backed worker as a direct conversation surface.",
27	  },
28	];
29	
30	type SessionState = {
31	  sessionId: string;
32	  cwd: string;
33	  mode: ConversationMode;
34	  runId: string | null;
35	  abortController: AbortController | null;
36	  seenMessageIds: Set<string>;
37	  seenEventIds: Set<string>;
38	  workerTextById: Map<string, string>;
39	};
40	
41	type WaitForTurnResult = {
42	  stopReason: acp.StopReason;
43	};
44	
45	type WaitForTurnArgs = {
46	  sessionId: string;
47	  runId: string;
48	  signal: AbortSignal;
49	  emit: (text: string) => Promise<void>;
50	  state?: SessionState;
51	};
52	
53	export type OmniHarnessAcpAgentDeps = {
54	  createConversation: typeof createConversation;
55	  sendConversationMessage: typeof sendConversationMessage;
56	  waitForTurn: (args: WaitForTurnArgs) => Promise<WaitForTurnResult>;
57	};
58	
59	function isConversationMode(value: string): value is ConversationMode {
60	  return CONVERSATION_MODES.includes(value as ConversationMode);
61	}
62	
63	function normalizeRunMode(value: string): ConversationMode {
64	  return isConversationMode(value) ? value : "implementation";
65	}
66	
67	function modeState(currentModeId: ConversationMode): acp.SessionModeState {
68	  const visibleModeId = currentModeId === "commit" ? "direct" : currentModeId;
69	  return {
70	    currentModeId: visibleModeId,
71	    availableModes: OMNI_ACP_MODES,
72	  };
73	}
74	
75	function textFromPrompt(prompt: acp.PromptRequest["prompt"]) {
76	  const chunks: string[] = [];
77	  for (const block of prompt) {
78	    if (block.type === "text" && block.text.trim()) {
79	      chunks.push(block.text.trim());
80	    } else if (block.type === "resource_link") {
81	      chunks.push(`Resource: ${block.uri}`);
82	    }
83	  }
84	  return chunks.join("\n\n").trim();
85	}
86	
87	function parseEventSummary(details: string | null, fallback: string) {
88	  if (!details?.trim()) {
89	    return fallback;
90	  }
91	
92	  try {
93	    const parsed = JSON.parse(details) as Record<string, unknown>;
94	    for (const key of ["summary", "reason", "error"]) {
95	      const value = parsed[key];
96	      if (typeof value === "string" && value.trim()) {
97	        return value.trim();
98	      }
99	    }
100	  } catch {
101	    return details.trim();
102	  }
103	
104	  return fallback;
105	}
106	
107	function activeWorkerStatus(status: string) {
108	  return status === "starting" || status === "working" || status === "running";
109	}
110	
111	function sessionInfoFromRun(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect): acp.SessionInfo {
112	  return {
113	    sessionId: run.id,
114	    cwd: run.projectPath || process.cwd(),
115	    title: run.title || run.id,
116	    updatedAt: run.updatedAt.toISOString(),
117	  };
118	}
119	
120	function waitForNotificationOrAbort(signal: AbortSignal, timeoutMs: number) {
121	  if (signal.aborted) {
122	    return Promise.resolve();
123	  }
124	
125	  return Promise.race([
126	    waitForEventStreamNotification(timeoutMs),
127	    new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
128	      signal.addEventListener("abort", () => resolve(), { once: true });
129	    }),
130	  ]);
131	}
132	
133	async function emitCurrentRunState(args: WaitForTurnArgs) {
134	  const state = args.state;
135	  if (!state) {
136	    return {
137	      run: await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId)).get(),
138	      workerRecords: await db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, args.runId)),
139	    };
140	  }
141	
142	  const [run, runMessages, workerRecords, runEvents] = await Promise.all([
143	    db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, args.runId)).get(),
144	    db.select().from(messages).where(eq(messages.runId, args.runId)).orderBy(asc(messages.createdAt), asc(messages.id)),
145	    db.select().from(workers).where(eq(workers.runId, args.runId)).orderBy(asc(workers.createdAt), asc(workers.id)),
146	    listExecutionEventsForRun(args.runId),
147	  ]);
148	
149	  for (const message of runMessages) {
150	    if (state.seenMessageIds.has(message.id)) {
151	      continue;
152	    }
153	    state.seenMessageIds.add(message.id);
154	    await args.emit(`${message.role}${message.workerId ? `:${message.workerId}` : ""}: ${message.content}`);
155	  }
156	
157	  for (const event of runEvents) {
158	    if (state.seenEventIds.has(event.id)) {
159	      continue;
160	    }
161	    state.seenEventIds.add(event.id);
162	    await args.emit(`${event.eventType}: ${parseEventSummary(event.details, event.eventType)}`);
163	  }
164	
165	  for (const worker of workerRecords) {
166	    const text = worker.currentText.trim() || worker.lastText.trim() || worker.outputLog.trim();
167	    if (!text || state.workerTextById.get(worker.id) === text) {
168	      continue;
169	    }
170	    state.workerTextById.set(worker.id, text);
171	    await args.emit(`worker:${worker.id}: ${text}`);
172	  }
173	
174	  return { run, workerRecords };
175	}
176	
177	export async function waitForOmniConversationTurn(args: WaitForTurnArgs): Promise<WaitForTurnResult> {
178	  while (!args.signal.aborted) {
179	    const snapshot = await emitCurrentRunState(args);
180	    if (!snapshot.run) {
181	      await args.emit(`Run ${args.runId} disappeared.`);
182	      return { stopReason: "end_turn" };
183	    }
184	
185	    if (snapshot.run.status === "failed") {
186	      await args.emit(`Run failed: ${snapshot.run.lastError || snapshot.run.id}`);
187	      return { stopReason: "refusal" };
188	    }
189	
190	    if (snapshot.run.status === "done") {
191	      await args.emit(`Run completed: ${snapshot.run.title || snapshot.run.id}`);
192	      return { stopReason: "end_turn" };
193	    }
194	
195	    // An Omni run is mode === "implementation" for its whole life, so keying
196	    // off mode alone would hang the ACP turn on the dormant supervisor during
197	    // the planning phase. Treat a planning-phase run like a single-worker run:
198	    // end the turn once its worker goes idle.
199	    const supervised = snapshot.run.mode === "implementation" && snapshot.run.phase !== "planning";
200	    if (!supervised && !snapshot.workerRecords.some((worker) => activeWorkerStatus(worker.status))) {
201	      return { stopReason: "end_turn" };
202	    }
203	
204	    await waitForNotificationOrAbort(args.signal, 1_000);
205	  }
206	
207	  return { stopReason: "cancelled" };
208	}
209	
210	export class OmniHarnessAcpAgent implements acp.Agent {
211	  private readonly sessions = new Map<string, SessionState>();
212	  private readonly deps: OmniHarnessAcpAgentDeps;
213	
214	  constructor(
215	    private readonly connection: Pick<acp.AgentSideConnection, "sessionUpdate">,
216	    deps: Partial<OmniHarnessAcpAgentDeps> = {},
217	  ) {
218	    this.deps = {
219	      createConversation,
220	      sendConversationMessage,
221	      waitForTurn: waitForOmniConversationTurn,
222	      ...deps,
223	    };
224	  }
225	
226	  async initialize(_params: acp.InitializeRequest): Promise<acp.InitializeResponse> {
227	    return {
228	      protocolVersion: acp.PROTOCOL_VERSION,
229	      agentInfo: {
230	        name: "OmniHarness",
231	        version: "0.1.0",
232	      },
233	      agentCapabilities: {
234	        loadSession: true,
235	        promptCapabilities: {},
236	        sessionCapabilities: {
237	          list: {},
238	          resume: {},
239	          fork: {},
240	        },
241	      },
242	    };
243	  }
244	
245	  async newSession(params: acp.NewSessionRequest): Promise<acp.NewSessionResponse> {
246	    const sessionId = createShortUuid();
247	    this.sessions.set(sessionId, {
248	      sessionId,
249	      cwd: params.cwd,
250	      mode: "implementation",
251	      runId: null,
252	      abortController: null,
253	      seenMessageIds: new Set(),
254	      seenEventIds: new Set(),
255	      workerTextById: new Map(),
256	    });
257	
258	    return {
259	      sessionId,
260	      modes: modeState("implementation"),
261	    };
262	  }
263	
264	  async loadSession(params: acp.LoadSessionRequest): Promise<acp.LoadSessionResponse> {
265	    const run = await this.requireRun(params.sessionId);
266	    const state = this.createSessionStateFromRun(run, params.cwd);
267	    this.sessions.set(params.sessionId, state);
268	    await this.replayRunHistory(params.sessionId);
269	
270	    return {
271	      modes: modeState(state.mode),
272	    };
273	  }
274	
275	  async unstable_listSessions(params: acp.ListSessionsRequest = {}): Promise<acp.ListSessionsResponse> {
276	    const offset = params.cursor ? Number.parseInt(params.cursor, 10) : 0;
277	    const safeOffset = Number.isFinite(offset) && offset > 0 ? offset : 0;
278	    const pageSize = 50;
279	    const allRuns = await db.select().from(runs).orderBy(desc(runs.updatedAt), desc(runs.id));
280	    const cwd = params.cwd?.trim() || null;
281	    const filteredRuns = cwd
282	      ? allRuns.filter((run) => (run.projectPath || process.cwd()) === cwd)
283	      : allRuns;
284	    const page = filteredRuns.slice(safeOffset, safeOffset + pageSize);
285	    const nextOffset = safeOffset + pageSize;
286	
287	    return {
288	      sessions: page.map(sessionInfoFromRun),
289	      nextCursor: nextOffset < filteredRuns.length ? String(nextOffset) : null,
290	    };
291	  }
292	
293	  async unstable_resumeSession(params: acp.ResumeSessionRequest): Promise<acp.ResumeSessionResponse> {
294	    const run = await this.requireRun(params.sessionId);
295	    const state = this.createSessionStateFromRun(run, params.cwd);
296	    this.sessions.set(params.sessionId, state);
297	
298	    return {
299	      modes: modeState(state.mode),
300	    };
301	  }
302	
303	  async unstable_forkSession(params: acp.ForkSessionRequest): Promise<acp.ForkSessionResponse> {
304	    const source = await this.resolveSessionOrRun(params.sessionId);
305	    const sessionId = createShortUuid();
306	    this.sessions.set(sessionId, {
307	      sessionId,
308	      cwd: params.cwd || source.cwd,
309	      mode: source.mode,
310	      runId: null,
311	      abortController: null,
312	      seenMessageIds: new Set(),
313	      seenEventIds: new Set(),
314	      workerTextById: new Map(),
315	    });
316	
317	    return {
318	      sessionId,
319	      modes: modeState(source.mode),
320	    };
321	  }
322	
323	  async setSessionMode(params: acp.SetSessionModeRequest): Promise<acp.SetSessionModeResponse> {
324	    const session = this.requireSession(params.sessionId);
325	    if (!isConversationMode(params.modeId)) {
326	      throw new Error(`Unsupported OmniHarness mode "${params.modeId}".`);
327	    }
328	    session.mode = params.modeId;
329	    await this.connection.sessionUpdate({
330	      sessionId: params.sessionId,
331	      update: {
332	        sessionUpdate: "current_mode_update",
333	        currentModeId: params.modeId,
334	      },
335	    });
336	    return {};
337	  }
338	
339	  async prompt(params: acp.PromptRequest): Promise<acp.PromptResponse> {
340	    const session = this.requireSession(params.sessionId);
341	    const command = textFromPrompt(params.prompt);
342	    if (!command) {
343	      throw new Error("OmniHarness ACP prompts must include text content.");
344	    }
345	
346	    session.abortController?.abort();
347	    const abortController = new AbortController();
348	    session.abortController = abortController;
349	
350	    const emit = (text: string) => this.emitText(params.sessionId, text);
351	
352	    try {
353	      if (!session.runId) {
354	        const created = await this.deps.createConversation({
355	          mode: session.mode,
356	          command,
357	          projectPath: session.cwd,
358	        });
359	        session.runId = created.runId;
360	        await emit(`Started ${created.mode} OmniHarness conversation ${created.runId}.`);
361	      } else {
362	        await this.deps.sendConversationMessage({
363	          runId: session.runId,
364	          content: command,
365	        });
366	      }
367	
368	      return await this.deps.waitForTurn({
369	        sessionId: params.sessionId,
370	        runId: session.runId,
371	        signal: abortController.signal,
372	        emit,
373	        state: session,
374	      });
375	    } finally {
376	      if (session.abortController === abortController) {
377	        session.abortController = null;
378	      }
379	    }
380	  }
381	
382	  async cancel(params: acp.CancelNotification) {
383	    const session = this.sessions.get(params.sessionId);
384	    session?.abortController?.abort();
385	  }
386	
387	  async authenticate(_params: acp.AuthenticateRequest) {
388	    return {};
389	  }
390	
391	  private requireSession(sessionId: string) {
392	    const session = this.sessions.get(sessionId);
393	    if (!session) {
394	      throw new Error(`OmniHarness ACP session not found: ${sessionId}`);
395	    }
396	    return session;
397	  }
398	
399	  private async requireRun(runId: string) {
400	    const run = await db.select().from(runs).where(eq(runs.id, runId)).get();
401	    if (!run) {
402	      throw new Error(`OmniHarness run not found: ${runId}`);
403	    }
404	    return run;
405	  }
406	
407	  private createSessionStateFromRun(run: typeof runs.$inferSelect, cwd: string): SessionState {
408	    return {
409	      sessionId: run.id,
410	      cwd: run.projectPath || cwd,
411	      mode: normalizeRunMode(run.mode),
412	      runId: run.id,
413	      abortController: null,
414	      seenMessageIds: new Set(),
415	      seenEventIds: new Set(),
416	      workerTextById: new Map(),
417	    };
418	  }
419	
420	  private async resolveSessionOrRun(sessionId: string) {
421	    const existingSession = this.sessions.get(sessionId);
422	    if (existingSession) {
423	      return {
424	        cwd: existingSession.cwd,
425	        mode: existingSession.mode,
426	      };
427	    }
428	
429	    const run = await this.requireRun(sessionId);
430	    return {
431	      cwd: run.projectPath || process.cwd(),
432	      mode: normalizeRunMode(run.mode),
433	    };
434	  }
435	
436	  private async replayRunHistory(sessionId: string) {
437	    const state = this.requireSession(sessionId);
438	    const runMessages = await db
439	      .select()
440	      .from(messages)
441	      .where(eq(messages.runId, state.runId || sessionId))
442	      .orderBy(asc(messages.createdAt), asc(messages.id));
443	
444	    for (const message of runMessages) {
445	      state.seenMessageIds.add(message.id);
446	      await this.connection.sessionUpdate({
447	        sessionId,
448	        update: {
449	          sessionUpdate: message.role === "user" ? "user_message_chunk" : "agent_message_chunk",
450	          content: {
451	            type: "text",
452	            text: message.content,
453	          },
454	        },
455	      });
456	    }
457	  }
458	
459	  private async emitText(sessionId: string, text: string) {
460	    if (!text.trim()) {
461	      return;
462	    }
463	    await this.connection.sessionUpdate({
464	      sessionId,
465	      update: {
466	        sessionUpdate: "agent_message_chunk",
467	        content: {
468	          type: "text",
469	          text,
470	        },
471	      },
472	    });
473	  }
474	}
```

```text
  /**
   * Checks if a given absolute path is allowed for file system operations.
```

```text
1	# Gemini YOLO Needs CLI Approval Mode
2	
3	**Date:** 2026-06-30
4	**Context:** OmniHarness agent runtime, Gemini ACP workers, worker YOLO/full-access mode
5	**Symptom:** A Gemini worker in YOLO/full-access mode still failed tool execution with `Tool execution ... denied by policy`.
6	**Root Cause:** OmniHarness tracked the worker as `full-access` and auto-approved ACP permission callbacks, but default Gemini ACP processes were launched without Gemini's own startup-time YOLO approval mode. Gemini could therefore deny tools internally before OmniHarness had any permission request to approve.
7	**Fix:** Centralize Gemini default argv construction in `buildGeminiArgs`, add `--approval-mode yolo` for `full-access` and `danger-full-access`, and route that builder through both normal worker spawn and worker prewarm.
8	**Verification:** `pnpm exec vitest run tests/server/agent-runtime/http.test.ts tests/server/agent-runtime/gemini-args.test.ts --no-file-parallelism --testTimeout 30000 --hookTimeout 30000`; `pnpm typecheck`.
9	**Prevention:** For CLI-backed workers, treat OmniHarness session mode and provider CLI policy as separate layers. When adding or changing permission modes, verify the child process argv/env as well as ACP permission callback behavior, including prewarmed workers.
10	**Skill/Doc Updates:** No shared skill update needed; this is a project-specific runtime contract captured in the OmniHarness learnings.
11
```

## Claude · 2026-06-30T22:56:28.395Z

```text
---

# Implementation Plan: Square Aspect Ratio Style Previews

## Objective
Convert all caption template style preview visualizations (both loop videos and static images) to a square aspect ratio. Configure relevant, highly tailored custom sentences for each of the existing global style presets, and ensure the UI tiles and tests reflect the updated square layout.

---

## Key Files & Context

1. **`src/lib/caption-render/style-loop-preview.ts`**:
   - Contains `prepareStyleLoopPreviewFrame` and `paintStyleLoopPreviewFrame` which calculate canvas bounds and render looping captions.
   - Target container is currently based on `EDITOR_LAYOUT_BOUNDS` (9:16 vertical) aspect ratios.
2. **`src/lib/caption-render/style-thumbnail.ts`**:
   - Generates static style thumbnails. Sized according to the exact text bounding box, causing irregular aspect ratios.
3. **`src/server/db/global-style-presets.ts`**:
   - Defines the existing global presets: "Bold Yellow Karaoke", "Bansko Pop", "White Cloud", "Rounded Box Whisper", "Simply 3D Yellow", "Pink Balloon 3D", and "Neon Glow Pop".
4. **`src/server/db/seed.ts`**:
   - Seeds global style presets into the database. Needs to include the configured custom sample text field.
5. **UI Tiles (uses `aspect-[4/5]`)**:
   - `src/app/(app)/editor/[editId]/caption-style-preview.tsx` (`PresetTile`, `PresetResetTile`)
   - `src/app/(app)/editor/[editId]/caption-style-panel.tsx` (Current style preview)
   - `src/app/(app)/admin/admin-default-styles-panel.tsx` (New default style button)
6. **`tests/admin-default-styles-panel.test.tsx`**:
   - Tests the admin default styles panel. Includes assertions checking for `aspect-[4/5]`.

---

## Proposed Solution

### 1. Square Canvas + Center-Aligned Rendering
Rather than stretching or changing the underlying layout coordinates system (which is strictly 1080x1920 9:16), we can continue to compute standard caption box layouts but scale and paint them onto a square canvas.
- Introduce a **padding multiplier** of `0.9` (so 90% of the square's width is filled by the caption bounding box, leaving a beautiful 5% margin on both the left and right sides).
- Use an offscreen canvas to render the caption bounding box at its exact layout aspect ratio, and then draw that offscreen canvas centered vertically and horizontally on the main square canvas:
  - `x = (square_size - scaled_width) / 2`
  - `y = (square_size - scaled_height) / 2`
- Cache/reuse the offscreen canvas inside `PreparedStyleLoopPreviewFrame` to avoid garbage collection pressure during live browser playback (24 FPS).
```
