## Claude tool result: toolu_01FJZK8vLRHVrzJcJY1p67DM · 2026-06-30T22:59:29.458Z

```text
# Implementation Plan: Square Aspect Ratio Style Previews
## Objective
Convert all caption template style preview visualizations (both loop videos and static images) to a square aspect ratio. Configure relevant, highly tailored custom sentences for each of the existing global style presets, and ensure the UI tiles and tests reflect the updated square layout.
## Key Files & Context
1. **`src/lib/caption-render/style-loop-preview.ts`**:
   - Contains `prepareStyleLoopPreviewFrame` and `paintStyleLoopPreviewFrame` which calculate canvas bounds and render looping captions.
   - Target container is currently based on `EDITOR_LAYOUT_BOUNDS` (9:16 vertical) aspect ratios.
2. **`src/lib/caption-render/style-thumbnail.ts`**:
   - Generates static style thumbnails. Sized according to the exact text bounding box, causing irregular aspect ratios.
3. **`src/server/db/global-style-presets.ts`**:
   - Defines the existing global presets: "Bold Yellow Karaoke", "Bansko Pop", "White Cloud", "Rounded Box Whisper", "Simply 3D Yellow", "Pink Balloon 3D", and "Neon Glow Pop".
4. **`src/server/db/seed.ts`**:
   - Seeds global style presets into the database. Needs to include the configured custom sample text field.
5. **UI Tiles (uses `aspect-[4/5]`)**:
   - `src/app/(app)/editor/[editId]/caption-style-preview.tsx` (`PresetTile`, `PresetResetTile`)
   - `src/app/(app)/editor/[editId]/caption-style-panel.tsx` (Current style preview)
   - `src/app/(app)/admin/admin-default-styles-panel.tsx` (New default style button)
6. **`tests/admin-default-styles-panel.test.tsx`**:
   - Tests the admin default styles panel. Includes assertions checking for `aspect-[4/5]`.
## Proposed Solution
### 1. Square Canvas + Center-Aligned Rendering
Rather than stretching or changing the underlying layout coordinates system (which is strictly 1080x1920 9:16), we can continue to compute standard caption box layouts but scale and paint them onto a square canvas.
- Introduce a **padding multiplier** of `0.9` (so 90% of the square's width is filled by the caption bounding box, leaving a beautiful 5% margin on both the left and right sides).
- Use an offscreen canvas to render the caption bounding box at its exact layout aspect ratio, and then draw that offscreen canvas centered vertically and horizontally on the main square canvas:
  - `x = (square_size - scaled_width) / 2`
  - `y = (square_size - scaled_height) / 2`
- Cache/reuse the offscreen canvas inside `PreparedStyleLoopPreviewFrame` to avoid garbage collection pressure during live browser playback (24 FPS).
### 2. Relevant Sentences per Style
Configure distinct, high-quality custom sentences tailored for each preset:
- **Bold Yellow Karaoke**: `"Sing your heart out tonight"`
- **Bansko Pop**: `"Powder day at Bansko!"`
- **White Cloud**: `"Soft like a summer cloud"`
- **Rounded Box Whisper**: `"A quiet moment in the forest"`
- **Simply 3D Yellow**: `"Pure yellow energy!"`
- **Pink Balloon 3D**: `"Floating high on pink balloons"`
- **Neon Glow Pop**: `"Glow bright under the neon light"`
## Detailed Implementation Steps
### Step 1: Update `src/lib/caption-render/style-loop-preview.ts`
- Modify `prepareStyleLoopPreviewFrame` to:
  - Use `const paddingMultiplier = 0.9`.
  - Compute uniform `scale = (targetWidth * paddingMultiplier) / Math.max(1, probe.previewSize.width)`.
  - Create and attach `offscreenCanvas` if `typeof document !== "undefined"`.
  - Set `width: targetWidth` and `height: targetWidth` to enforce a square.
- Modify `paintStyleLoopPreviewFrame` to:
  - Fill a background color (`#0f1216`) on the square canvas.
  - Draw using `offscreenCanvas` (or dynamic fallback) centered inside the square canvas:
    - `x = (prepared.width - scaledW) / 2`
    - `y = (prepared.height - scaledH) / 2`
### Step 2: Update `src/lib/caption-render/style-thumbnail.ts`
- Update `generateStylePreviewDataUrl` to follow the exact same square aspect ratio logic:
  - Set container size `containerSize = TARGET_WIDTH`.
  - Use `scale = (containerSize * 0.9) / boxWidthLayout`.
  - Create `mainCanvas` with size `containerSize` x `containerSize`.
  - Create `offscreenCanvas` with size `scaledW` x `scaledH`.
  - Paint into `offscreenCanvas` and draw onto `mainCanvas` centered.
### Step 3: Update Preset Seeds and Seeding Script
- Update `GlobalStylePresetSeed` interface in `src/server/db/global-style-presets.ts` to make `previewSampleText` an optional string.
- Add tailored sample sentences (listed above) under `previewSampleText` for each of the seven global presets.
- Update `src/server/db/seed.ts` to set `previewSampleText` and `previewVideo` during both inserts and updates.
### Step 4: Update CSS aspect ratios in UI
- Search for `aspect-[4/5]` in style preview components and replace them with `aspect-square`:
  - `src/app/(app)/editor/[editId]/caption-style-preview.tsx` (for both `PresetTile` and `PresetResetTile`)
  - `src/app/(app)/editor/[editId]/caption-style-panel.tsx`
  - `src/app/(app)/admin/admin-default-styles-panel.tsx`
### Step 5: Update Tests
- Update `tests/admin-default-styles-panel.test.tsx` to assert `aspect-square` instead of `aspect-[4/5]`.
Please let me know if you agree with this proposed strategy and implementation details, or if you would like any adjustments before we proceed!
```

## Claude · 2026-06-30T23:00:05.478Z

Two things are now done:

**Root cause fixed:** The Gemini CLI was failing on two levels:
1. Writing to `docs/plans` was blocked — Gemini's Plan Mode restricts `write_file` to the designated plans directory (the session's tmp dir)
2. Writing to the tmp plans dir failed with "Internal error" — Gemini's internal file system service couldn't handle that path

**Fix applied:** Replaced the symlinked `settings.json` at `/Users/masterman/NLP/cloudcaptions/.omniharness/cli-home/gemini/.gemini/settings.json` with a real project-specific file that sets `general.plan.directory = "docs/plans"`. Now when Gemini enters Plan Mode in any cloudcaptions session, it writes plans directly to `docs/plans` — which is in the project workspace, writeable, and correctly detected by OmniHarness's artifact scanner.

**Session 4b575ebbc2fe:** The session is already `done`. Gemini completed all the research and formulated a full plan but couldn't persist it. I've saved it to `/Users/masterman/NLP/cloudcaptions/docs/plans/009-square-aspect-ratio-style-previews.md` — it's ready to implement.
